- **speech-paper-ready-reviewer**: 当 Codex/Agent 进入论文 pipeline 第 3 步 review，且需要把已经筛选过的语音/音频 arXiv 论文从 selected_papers.json、firstpage、fulltext、PDF 证据转成独立 review result 和 reviewed.jsonl 记录时，必须使用这个 skill。触发场景包括“精读 selected_papers”、“补 reviewed.jsonl”、“读 PDF 写 review”、“把 fulltext 转成 ready review”，以及任何要求执行 papers 项目第 3 步的任务；也兼容英文同义指令。review 阶段应由主 Agent 调度，每篇论文使用独立子 Agent按本 skill 精读并写入 manifest 指定的单篇结果文件，再由 finalizer 合并。这个 skill 是 review 阶段的运行时规则来源，不需要额外规格文档。它不负责 collect、filter、下载 PDF、渲染 Markdown 或写入 Obsidian。 ([South-Twilight/speech-paper-daily](https://github.com/South-Twilight/speech-paper-daily/tree/HEAD/skills/speech-paper-ready-reviewer) · ★1)

- **speed-up**: Shortens the path to the current session's objectives without changing them. Maps the remaining work as a dependency tree (what each task waits on, which files it writes, which user decisions it needs) and finds the safe speed-ups: tasks that can start earlier, independent tasks that can run in parallel as sub-agents, waits such as CI that can overlap other work, and tasks already done.
